# RPGACE UI audit (UA-1)

Generated 2026-11-04 15:09. Every screen reachable by tapping, from login, at every depth. Sample data (2 rows per list, shaped from each screen's own query); phone width 390px; clock 2026-11-04 12:00.

Legend: 📄 page · 🪟 pop-up · → where a button leads · ↻ changes the same screen · · nothing visible happened · ⚠ error message.

## Totals

- Screens found: **726** (30 pages, 696 pop-ups)
- Side-menu destinations: **26**
- Buttons tapped: **5431**
- Complete: **yes** - every screen reached by tapping was opened and every button on it tapped

### Not covered by a tap crawl
Swipes and drags (day-plan block drag, swipe between pages), long-press, typed input that changes what shows, and time-of-day states other than 12:00 on Nov 4 (morning wake screen, evening review, during a shift). Lists show 2 sample rows each, so screens that only appear with many rows or a specific record are not reached.

### Opens by itself (not from a button)
- Work page (workMode.ui.autoOpen) - during a shift, once per shift
- What happened? card (myDay.ui._maybeMissed) - after a block passes with nothing logged
- Good morning screen - after the wake alarm / I'm up
- Evening review - from Going to bed

## 1. Login

- Shows: ⚔ RPGACE ENTER YOUR ACCESS KEY ENTER THE REALM Show / Hide password
- Inputs: •••••••••••••• · Buttons: ENTER THE REALM → Dashboard
